+1 for tedsb16, it is skies of Arcadia for Dreamcast, I believe it was re-released on gamecube, right? Don't count re-releases unless specified in the question, though the word re-release can be argued. As rule 12 states, I can be wrong, feel free to point that out. Just be gentle. I have a fragile ego :D +2 for Galdion. It was also on SNES.BLITZ! There is now a 10 8 question blitz on old school games made new! Check out that section for the special rules and screens. DO NOT GUESS MORE THAN TWO OF THE BLITZ QUESTIONS AT ONCE!Inspired by the way Channel28 does it in the Marvel/DC Quiz (though he does it to span both developers) I've decided to post two questions at a time. I think this will allow things to move more quickly and give more time for the harder ones. I'll try to balance it in a way that allows for this to flow faster by doing one older and one newer plus one multi-platform and one non-multi-platform, but it might not always work that way.RULES:
<input type="button" value="Show Spoiler!" style="width:100px;font-size:12px;margin:10px;padding:0px;" onclick="if (this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display != '') { this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display = ''; this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['hide'].style.display = 'none'; this.innerText = ''; this.value = 'Hide Spoiler!'; } else { this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display = 'none'; this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['hide'].style.display = ''; this.innerText = ''; this.value = 'Show Spoiler!'; }" />
1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

Feel free to pick one out every 5 points and PM me.http://dl.dropbox.com/u/12107848/NDS%20Downloaded.txtI also have some Humble Frozenbyte Bundle gift urls. These keys can no longer be acquired as this pack is now closed, so when you get 5 points, feel free to PM me with a request.

This is a new Blitz! similar to the last one. All of these screens are of PS1 remakes of old classics. Some are complete overhauls, others are really just graphical upgrades. Please note the special rules for this Blitz! event.SPECIAL RULES:1. System guesses will not count for this Blitz, all of these screens are from Playstation 1. Just give me a game title. Either the new title, or old title will do if there is a difference.2. There are 10 screenshots. Each person can guess at 2 of the screens once a day on top of the normal questions. That means you can make a total of 4 guesses if you attempt both of the general questions as well.3. You can come back the next day to guess two more. As far as time zones go, if I suspect that you did not wait a day, remember that your profile should tell me your local time and I may check that.4. Each screenshot is worth 1 point.5. Each screen is from a different game than the other screens within the blitz, though it may have been done before within our previous questions.6. Do specify which screens you are guessing at. This is very important since there are so many.7. As it progresses, I will remove the links that have been guessed correctly until all screens are guessed.SCREENS:OSQ1: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ1.jpgOSQ2: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ2.jpgOSQ3: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ3.jpgOSQ4: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ4.jpgOSQ5: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ5.jpgOSQ8: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ8.jpgOSQ9: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...0Blitz/OSQ9.jpgOSQ10: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/p...Blitz/OSQ10.jpg

+1 for Galdion the wise.BLITZ! There is now a 10 6 question blitz on old school games made new! Check out that section for the special rules and screens. DO NOT GUESS MORE THAN TWO OF THE BLITZ QUESTIONS AT ONCE!Inspired by the way Channel28 does it in the Marvel/DC Quiz (though he does it to span both developers) I've decided to post two questions at a time. I think this will allow things to move more quickly and give more time for the harder ones. I'll try to balance it in a way that allows for this to flow faster by doing one older and one newer plus one multi-platform and one non-multi-platform, but it might not always work that way.RULES:
<input type="button" value="Show Spoiler!" style="width:100px;font-size:12px;margin:10px;padding:0px;" onclick="if (this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display != '') { this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display = ''; this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['hide'].style.display = 'none'; this.innerText = ''; this.value = 'Hide Spoiler!'; } else { this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['show'].style.display = 'none'; this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName('div')['hide'].style.display = ''; this.innerText = ''; this.value = 'Show Spoiler!'; }" />
1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get the game title right, then I tell you that you were wrong on the system guess, you are allowed to offer another system guess, once for each time I correct you and only for the system if you have properly guessed the title.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Questions are worth one point. Extra Questions vary.5. If a game is said to be multi-platform, a bonus point will be awarded if you guess the console correctly along with a correct game answer.6. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.7. Games may be repeated.8. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.9. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.10. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.11. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.12. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.8. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.9. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.10. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.11. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.5. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.6. Games may be repeated.7. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.8. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.9. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.10. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.11. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.

1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.5. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.6. Games may be repeated.7. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.8. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.9. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.10. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.11. I am not infallible. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.5. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.6. Games may be repeated.7. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.8. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.9. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I still consider it to not be a multi-platform game. Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate cosoles.10. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.11. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, let me know and I'll fix it.INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though.
